## Raw second transcription

```yaml
```yaml
lines:
  - id: k1p.h_zero
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Zero"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ss.zero}
    cite: "Project constant"

  - id: k1p.h_b1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 1, ordinary business income (loss), zero if box 1 is blank"
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.has_b1, {const: true}]}
          then: k1p.box1
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.has_b1, {const: false}]}
          then: k1p.h_zero
    cite: "Schedule K-1 (Form 1065) 2025, Part III, box 1"

  - id: k1p.h_b2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 2, net rental real estate income (loss), zero if box 2 is blank"
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.has_b2, {const: true}]}
          then: k1p.box2
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.has_b2, {const: false}]}
          then: k1p.h_zero
    cite: "Schedule K-1 (Form 1065) 2025, Part III, box 2"

  - id: k1p.h_b3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 3, other net rental income (loss), zero if box 3 is blank"
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.has_b3, {const: true}]}
          then: k1p.box3
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.has_b3, {const: false}]}
          then: k1p.h_zero
    cite: "Schedule K-1 (Form 1065) 2025, Part III, box 3"

  - id: k1p.h_b4a
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 4a, guaranteed payments for services, zero if box 4a is blank"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.has_b4a, {const: true}]}
          then: k1p.box4a
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.has_b4a, {const: false}]}
          then: k1p.h_zero
    cite: "i1065sk1 2025 p.15, Box 4a"

  - id: k1p.h_b1_pos
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 1 income (zero if a loss)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: k1p.h_b1, take: {const: 0}}
    cite: "i1065sk1 2025 p.14, Box 1"

  - id: k1p.h_b1_neg
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 1 loss as a positive amount (zero if income)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: k1p.h_zero, take: k1p.h_b1}
    cite: "i1065sk1 2025 p.14, Box 1"

  - id: k1p.h_prior_b1_has
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Prior-year unallowed loss from the box 1 activity"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.prior_any, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.prior_b1_any, {const: true}]}
    cite: "i8582 2025 p.10, Part V, column (c)"

  - id: k1p.h_prior_b1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Prior-year unallowed loss, box 1 activity (2024 Form 8582, Part VII, column (c))"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_prior_b1_has, {const: true}]}
          then: k1p.prior_b1
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_prior_b1_has, {const: false}]}
          then: k1p.h_zero
    cite: "i8582 2025 p.10, Part V, column (c)"

  - id: k1p.h_prior_b2_has
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Prior-year unallowed loss from the box 2 activity"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.prior_any, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.prior_b2_any, {const: true}]}
    cite: "i8582 2025 p.10, Parts IV and V, column (c)"

  - id: k1p.h_prior_b2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Prior-year unallowed loss, box 2 activity (2024 Form 8582, Part VII, column (c))"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_prior_b2_has, {const: true}]}
          then: k1p.prior_b2
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_prior_b2_has, {const: false}]}
          then: k1p.h_zero
    cite: "i8582 2025 p.10, Parts IV and V, column (c)"

  - id: k1p.h_prior_b3_has
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Prior-year unallowed loss from the box 3 activity"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.prior_any, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.prior_b3_any, {const: true}]}
    cite: "i8582 2025 p.10, Part V, column (c)"

  - id: k1p.h_prior_b3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Prior-year unallowed loss, box 3 activity (2024 Form 8582, Part VII, column (c))"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_prior_b3_has, {const: true}]}
          then: k1p.prior_b3
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_prior_b3_has, {const: false}]}
          then: k1p.h_zero
    cite: "i8582 2025 p.10, Part V, column (c)"

  - id: k1p.h_b1_rel
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 1 activity has a current amount or a prior-year unallowed loss"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.has_b1, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_prior_b1_has, {const: true}]}
    cite: "i1065sk1 2025 p.14, Box 1"

  - id: k1p.h_mp
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "You materially participated in the partnership's trade or business activity"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [k1p.work, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [k1p.hours500, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.five_of_ten, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.t6, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [k1p.general, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [k1p.work, {const: true}]}
            - op: test
              any:
                - {op: test, eq: [k1p.nearly_all, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [k1p.most_hours, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [k1p.t4, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [k1p.t7, {const: true}]}
    cite: "i1065sk1 2025 p.9, Material participation (tests 1 to 7; limited partners tests 1, 5 or 6)"
    note: "A limited partner who owned a general partnership interest all year may use all seven tests; with no input for that case, item G (general partner or LLC member-manager) decides which tests apply."

  - id: k1p.h_b1_np
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 1 activity is nonpassive (material participation, or trading personal property for the account of owners)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_b1_rel, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [k1p.trader, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_mp, {const: true}]}
    cite: "i1065sk1 2025 pp.8-9, Passive Activity Limitations, Effect of determination; p.11, Special rules for certain other activities"

  - id: k1p.h_b1_inc_former
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Former passive activity: current year net income up to the prior-year unallowed loss"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [k1p.h_b1_pos, k1p.h_prior_b1]}
    cite: "i8582 2025 pp.8-9, Former Passive Activities"

  - id: k1p.h_show_b1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 1 activity is entered on Form 8582"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_b1_rel, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_b1_np, {const: false}]}
            - {op: test, gt: [k1p.h_prior_b1, {const: 0}]}
    cite: "i8582 2025 pp.8-10"

  - id: k1p.h_b2_rel
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 2 activity has a current amount or a prior-year unallowed loss"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.has_b2, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_prior_b2_has, {const: true}]}
    cite: "i1065sk1 2025 p.14, Box 2"

  - id: k1p.h_active
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "You actively participated in the rental real estate activity (Form 8582, Part IV)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_b2_rel, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.decisions, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.tenth, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.general, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, ne: [f1040.status, {const: "mfs"}]}
    cite: "i1065sk1 2025 pp.10-11, Special allowance for a rental real estate activity; i8582 2025 p.10, Part IV caution"
    note: "Limited partners can't actively participate. Married filing separately qualifies for Part IV only if the spouses lived apart all year, and no input says so. Chosen reading: on a married-filing-separately return the box 2 activity goes in Part V, which allows no special allowance."

  - id: k1p.h_prior_b2_in4
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 2 prior-year unallowed loss goes in Part IV (active participation in both the year the loss arose and this year)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_active, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_prior_b2_has, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.prior_not_active, {const: false}]}
    cite: "i8582 2025 p.10, Part IV"

  - id: k1p.h_prior_b2_p4
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 2 prior-year unallowed loss entered in Part IV, column (c)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_prior_b2_in4, {const: true}]}
          then: k1p.h_prior_b2
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_prior_b2_in4, {const: false}]}
          then: k1p.h_zero
    cite: "i8582 2025 p.10, Part IV, column (c)"

  - id: k1p.h_prior_b2_p5
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 2 prior-year unallowed loss entered in Part V, column (c)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: k1p.h_prior_b2, take: k1p.h_prior_b2_p4}
    cite: "i8582 2025 p.10, Part IV ('enter the prior-year unallowed loss in column (c) of Part V')"

  - id: k1p.h_b2_prior_same
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 2 prior-year unallowed loss entered in the same Part as the current year box 2 amount"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_active, {const: true}]}
          then: k1p.h_prior_b2_p4
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_active, {const: false}]}
          then: k1p.h_prior_b2
    cite: "i8582 2025 p.10, Parts IV and V"

  - id: k1p.h_b2_split
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 2 prior-year unallowed loss entered in Part V while the current year amount is in Part IV"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_active, {const: true}]}
          then: k1p.h_prior_b2_p5
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_active, {const: false}]}
          then: k1p.h_zero
    cite: "i8582 2025 p.10, Part IV"

  - id: k1p.h_show_b3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 3 activity is entered on Form 8582"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.has_b3, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_prior_b3_has, {const: true}]}
    cite: "i1065sk1 2025 pp.14-15, Box 3"

  - id: k1p.h_show_p5
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This partnership has an entry in Form 8582, Part V"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_show_b1, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_show_b3, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_b2_rel, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_active, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, gt: [k1p.h_b2_split, {const: 0}]}
    cite: "i8582 2025 p.10, Part V"

  - id: k1p.h_b1_lp
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 1 activity: current year net loss plus prior-year unallowed loss"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [k1p.b1_net_loss, k1p.h_prior_b1]}
    cite: "i8582 2025 p.10, Part V, columns (d) and (e)"

  - id: k1p.h_b2_lp
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 2 activity: current year net loss plus prior-year unallowed loss in the same Part"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [k1p.b2_net_loss, k1p.h_b2_prior_same]}
    cite: "i8582 2025 p.10, Parts IV and V, columns (d) and (e)"

  - id: k1p.h_b3_lp
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 3 activity: current year net loss plus prior-year unallowed loss"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [k1p.b3_net_loss, k1p.h_prior_b3]}
    cite: "i8582 2025 p.10, Part V, columns (d) and (e)"

  - id: k1p.h_b2_inc_p5
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 2 current year net income entered in Part V"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_active, {const: true}]}
          then: k1p.h_zero
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_active, {const: false}]}
          then: k1p.b2_net_income
    cite: "i8582 2025 p.10, Part V, column (a)"

  - id: k1p.h_b2_loss_p5
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 2 current year net loss entered in Part V"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_active, {const: true}]}
          then: k1p.h_zero
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_active, {const: false}]}
          then: k1p.b2_net_loss
    cite: "i8582 2025 p.10, Part V, column (b)"

  - id: k1p.h_b1_np_a
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 1 amount less the part entered on Form 8582 as income"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: subtract, from: k1p.h_b1, take: k1p.b1_net_income}
    cite: "i1065sk1 2025 p.14, Box 1, item 1; i8582 2025 pp.8-9, Former Passive Activities"

  - id: k1p.h_b1_np_amt
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Nonpassive box 1 income (loss)"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [k1p.h_b1_np_a, k1p.b1_net_loss]}
    cite: "i1065sk1 2025 p.14, Box 1, item 1"

  - id: k1p.h_np_pos
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Nonpassive box 1 income (zero if a loss)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: k1p.h_b1_np_amt, take: {const: 0}}
    cite: "i1065sk1 2025 p.14, Box 1, item 1"

  - id: k1p.h_any_loss
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "A loss is reported in box 1, 2, 3, 8 or 9a"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, lt: [k1p.h_b1, {const: 0}]}
        - {op: test, lt: [k1p.h_b2, {const: 0}]}
        - {op: test, lt: [k1p.h_b3, {const: 0}]}
        - {op: test, lt: [k1p.box8_if_any, {const: 0}]}
        - {op: test, lt: [k1p.box9a_if_any, {const: 0}]}
    cite: "i1065sk1 2025 p.3, Basis Limitations; p.8, At-Risk Limitations"

  - id: k1p.h_loss_gate
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "A loss is limited by the basis or at-risk rules (Form 6198)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_any_loss, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [k1p.basis_short, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [k1p.not_at_risk, {const: true}]}
    cite: "i1065sk1 2025 p.3, Basis Limitations; p.8, At-Risk Limitations"

  - id: k1p.h_dist_gate
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 19 distributions other than code A, or cash distributed more than basis (gain on Form 8949)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.has_b19, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [k1p.b19_other, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [k1p.dist_over, {const: true}]}
    cite: "i1065sk1 2025 p.25, Box 19, code A, Gain"

  - id: k1p.h_z_gate
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 20, code Z statement shows section 199A dividends or cooperative items"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.has_b20z, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.z_other, {const: true}]}
    cite: "i1065sk1 2025 pp.29-30, Box 20, code Z"

  - id: k1p.b1_net_income
    printed: "V (a) (k1p.b1_net_income)"
    label: "Form 8582, Part V, column (a): current year net income (box 1, ordinary business income)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_b1_np, {const: false}]}
          then: k1p.h_b1_pos
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_b1_np, {const: true}]}
          then: k1p.h_b1_inc_former
    refuses: [k1p.ptp, k1p.r_b2223]
    shown_when: k1p.h_show_b1
    cite: "i1065sk1 2025 p.14, Box 1, item 2a; i8582 2025 p.10, Part V, column (a); pp.8-9, Former Passive Activities"
    note: "For a former passive activity (box 1 now nonpassive, with a prior-year unallowed loss), the text says to enter the current year net income up to the prior-year unallowed loss on Form 8582. Chosen reading: that part is entered here and the rest is nonpassive."

  - id: k1p.b1_net_loss
    printed: "V (b) (k1p.b1_net_loss)"
    label: "Form 8582, Part V, column (b): current year net loss (box 1, ordinary business loss)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_b1_np, {const: false}]}
          then: k1p.h_b1_neg
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_b1_np, {const: true}]}
          then: k1p.h_zero
    refuses: [k1p.ptp, k1p.r_b2223, k1p.h_loss_gate]
    shown_when: k1p.h_show_b1
    cite: "i1065sk1 2025 p.14, Box 1, item 2b; i8582 2025 p.10, Part V, column (b); p.9 (a former passive activity's current year loss is not entered)"

  - id: k1p.b2_net_income
    printed: "IV/V (a)"
    label: "Form 8582, Part IV or V, column (a): current year net income (box 2, net rental real estate income)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: k1p.h_b2, take: {const: 0}}
    refuses: [k1p.ptp, k1p.r_b2223]
    shown_when: k1p.h_b2_rel
    cite: "i1065sk1 2025 p.14, Box 2; i8582 2025 p.10, Parts IV and V, column (a)"
    note: "Box 2 is passive unless you were a real estate professional who materially participated. There is no input for that case, so box 2 is always treated as passive."

  - id: k1p.b2_net_loss
    printed: "IV/V (b)"
    label: "Form 8582, Part IV or V, column (b): current year net loss (box 2, net rental real estate loss)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: k1p.h_zero, take: k1p.h_b2}
    refuses: [k1p.ptp, k1p.r_b2223, k1p.h_loss_gate]
    shown_when: k1p.h_b2_rel
    cite: "i1065sk1 2025 p.14, Box 2, items 1 and 2; i8582 2025 p.10, Parts IV and V, column (b)"
    note: "The exception of box 2 item 1 (a loss reported in column (g) without Form 8582 when all conditions a to h are met) is not applied. The loss is always routed through Form 8582, which item 2 allows."

  - id: k1p.b3_net_income
    printed: "V (a) (k1p.b3_net_income)"
    label: "Form 8582, Part V, column (a): current year net income (box 3, other net rental income)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: k1p.h_b3, take: {const: 0}}
    refuses: [k1p.ptp, k1p.r_b2223]
    shown_when: k1p.h_show_b3
    cite: "i1065sk1 2025 pp.14-15, Box 3; i8582 2025 p.10, Part V, column (a)"

  - id: k1p.b3_net_loss
    printed: "V (b) (k1p.b3_net_loss)"
    label: "Form 8582, Part V, column (b): current year net loss (box 3, other net rental loss)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: k1p.h_zero, take: k1p.h_b3}
    refuses: [k1p.ptp, k1p.r_b2223, k1p.h_loss_gate]
    shown_when: k1p.h_show_b3
    cite: "i1065sk1 2025 pp.14-15, Box 3; i8582 2025 p.10, Part V, column (b)"

  - id: k1p.b1_overall_loss
    printed: "(e) (k1p.b1_overall_loss)"
    label: "Form 8582, Part V, column (e): overall loss (box 1 activity)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: k1p.h_b1_lp, take: k1p.b1_net_income}
    shown_when: k1p.h_show_b1
    cite: "i8582 2025 p.10, Part V, columns (d) and (e)"

  - id: k1p.b2_overall_loss
    printed: "(e) (k1p.b2_overall_loss)"
    label: "Form 8582, Part IV or V, column (e): overall loss (box 2 activity)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: k1p.h_b2_lp, take: k1p.b2_net_income}
    shown_when: k1p.h_b2_rel
    cite: "i8582 2025 p.10, Parts IV and V, columns (d) and (e)"
    note: "When the current amount is in Part IV but the prior-year unallowed loss must go in Part V (no active participation in the year it arose), the prior loss forms a separate Part V row (k1p.h_b2_split) and is left out of this column (e)."

  - id: k1p.b3_overall_loss
    printed: "(e) (k1p.b3_overall_loss)"
    label: "Form 8582, Part V, column (e): overall loss (box 3 activity)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: k1p.h_b3_lp, take: k1p.b3_net_income}
    shown_when: k1p.h_show_b3
    cite: "i8582 2025 p.10, Part V, columns (d) and (e)"

  - id: k1p.p4_income
    printed: "IV (a)"
    label: "Form 8582, Part IV, column (a): current year net income"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_active, {const: true}]}
          then: k1p.b2_net_income
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_active, {const: false}]}
          then: k1p.h_zero
    shown_when: k1p.h_active
    cite: "i8582 2025 p.10, Part IV, column (a)"

  - id: k1p.p4_loss
    printed: "IV (b)"
    label: "Form 8582, Part IV, column (b): current year net loss"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_active, {const: true}]}
          then: k1p.b2_net_loss
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_active, {const: false}]}
          then: k1p.h_zero
    shown_when: k1p.h_active
    cite: "i8582 2025 p.10, Part IV, column (b)"

  - id: k1p.p4_prior
    printed: "IV (c)"
    label: "Form 8582, Part IV, column (c): prior year unallowed loss"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: k1p.h_prior_b2_p4}
    shown_when: k1p.h_active
    cite: "i8582 2025 p.10, Part IV, column (c)"

  - id: k1p.p6_loss
    printed: "VI (a)"
    label: "Form 8582, Part VI, column (a): overall loss from column (e) of Part IV"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_active, {const: true}]}
          then: k1p.b2_overall_loss
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_active, {const: false}]}
          then: k1p.h_zero
    shown_when: k1p.h_active
    cite: "i8582 2025 p.12, Part VI, column (a)"
    note: "Part VI is completed only when Part II, line 9 is not zero, which is decided on the return as a whole. This line gives the entry the activity would have, and shows it whenever the activity is in Part IV."

  - id: k1p.p5_income
    printed: "V (a) (k1p.p5_income)"
    label: "Form 8582, Part V, column (a): current year net income (this partnership's activities)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [k1p.b1_net_income, k1p.b3_net_income, k1p.h_b2_inc_p5]}
    shown_when: k1p.h_show_p5
    cite: "i8582 2025 p.10, Part V, column (a)"

  - id: k1p.p5_loss
    printed: "V (b) (k1p.p5_loss)"
    label: "Form 8582, Part V, column (b): current year net loss (this partnership's activities)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [k1p.b1_net_loss, k1p.b3_net_loss, k1p.h_b2_loss_p5]}
    shown_when: k1p.h_show_p5
    cite: "i8582 2025 p.10, Part V, column (b)"

  - id: k1p.p5_prior
    printed: "V (c)"
    label: "Form 8582, Part V, column (c): prior year unallowed loss (this partnership's activities)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [k1p.h_prior_b1, k1p.h_prior_b3, k1p.h_prior_b2_p5]}
    shown_when: k1p.h_show_p5
    cite: "i8582 2025 p.10, Part V, column (c)"

  - id: k1p.col_h
    printed: "28 (h)"
    label: "Schedule E, line 28, column (h): passive income"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [k1p.b1_net_income, k1p.b2_net_income, k1p.b3_net_income]}
    shown_when: k1p.sche_row
    cite: "i1065sk1 2025 p.14, Box 1 item 2a, Box 2 item 4; p.15, Box 3; i1040se 2025 p.9"
    note: "The income of a former passive activity, up to its prior-year unallowed loss, is entered on Form 8582. Chosen reading: report that income in column (h)."

  - id: k1p.col_k
    printed: "28 (k)"
    label: "Schedule E, line 28, column (k): nonpassive income"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [k1p.h_b4a, k1p.h_np_pos]}
    shown_when: k1p.sche_row
    cite: "i1065sk1 2025 p.14, Box 1 item 1; p.15, Box 4a"
    note: "Box 1 says report nonpassive amounts in column (i) or (k). Income goes in column (k) and losses in column (i)."

  - id: k1p.col_i
    printed: "28 (i)"
    label: "Schedule E, line 28, column (i): nonpassive loss"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: k1p.h_zero, take: k1p.h_b1_np_amt}
    refuses: [k1p.h_loss_gate]
    shown_when: k1p.sche_row
    cite: "i1065sk1 2025 p.14, Box 1 item 1 (after applying the basis and at-risk limitations)"

  - id: k1p.one
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "One Schedule K-1"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: param, ref: ss.one}
    cite: "Project constant"

  - id: k1p.box5_if_any
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 5, interest income (Form 1040, line 2b), zero if blank"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.has_b5, {const: true}]}
          then: k1p.box5
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.has_b5, {const: false}]}
          then: k1p.h_zero
    cite: "i1065sk1 2025 p.15, Box 5"

  - id: k1p.box6a_if_any
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 6a, ordinary dividends (Form 1040, line 3b), zero if blank"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.has_b6a, {const: true}]}
          then: k1p.box6a
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.has_b6a, {const: false}]}
          then: k1p.h_zero
    cite: "i1065sk1 2025 p.15, Box 6a"

  - id: k1p.box6b_if_any
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 6b, qualified dividends (Form 1040, line 3a), zero if blank"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.has_b6a, {const: true}]}
          then: k1p.box6b
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.has_b6a, {const: false}]}
          then: k1p.h_zero
    cite: "i1065sk1 2025 p.15, Box 6b"
    note: "Qualified dividends are part of ordinary dividends. When box 6a is blank, box 6b is taken as zero."

  - id: k1p.box8_if_any
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 8, net short-term capital gain (loss) (Schedule D, line 5), zero if blank"
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.has_b8, {const: true}]}
          then: k1p.box8
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.has_b8, {const: false}]}
          then: k1p.h_zero
    cite: "i1065sk1 2025 p.15, Box 8"

  - id: k1p.box9a_if_any
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 9a, net long-term capital gain (loss) (Schedule D, line 12), zero if blank"
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.has_b9a, {const: true}]}
          then: k1p.box9a
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.has_b9a, {const: false}]}
          then: k1p.h_zero
    cite: "i1065sk1 2025 p.15, Box 9a"

  - id: k1p.box9b_if_any
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 9b, collectibles (28%) gain (28% Rate Gain Worksheet, line 4), zero if blank"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.has_b9bc, {const: true}]}
          then: k1p.box9b
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.has_b9bc, {const: false}]}
          then: k1p.h_zero
    cite: "i1065sk1 2025 p.16, Box 9b"

  - id: k1p.box9c_if_any
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 9c, unrecaptured section 1250 gain (Unrecaptured Section 1250 Gain Worksheet, line 5), zero if blank"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.has_b9bc, {const: true}]}
          then: k1p.box9c
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.has_b9bc, {const: false}]}
          then: k1p.h_zero
    cite: "i1065sk1 2025 p.16, Box 9c"

  - id: k1p.box14a_if_any
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 14, code A, net earnings (loss) from self-employment, zero if blank"
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.has_b14a, {const: true}]}
          then: k1p.box14a
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.has_b14a, {const: false}]}
          then: k1p.h_zero
    cite: "i1065sk1 2025 p.22, Box 14, code A"

  - id: k1p.se_pos
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 14, code A, net earnings from self-employment (positive amount) for Schedule SE"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: k1p.box14a_if_any, take: {const: 0}}
    cite: "i1065sk1 2025 p.22, Box 14, code A"
    note: "For a loss, only the deductible amount goes on Schedule SE, and the text does not let the tool determine it here, so only the positive amount is given. A general partner's section 179 reduction is screened out (r_b12). Unreimbursed partnership expenses and oil and gas depletion have no inputs and are not subtracted."

  - id: k1p.box15o_if_any
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 15, code O, backup withholding (Form 1040, line 25c), zero if blank"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.has_b15o, {const: true}]}
          then: k1p.box15o
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.has_b15o, {const: false}]}
          then: k1p.h_zero
    cite: "i1065sk1 2025 p.23, Box 15, code O"

  - id: k1p.box18a_if_any
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 18, code A, tax-exempt interest income (Form 1040, line 2a), zero if blank"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.has_b18a, {const: true}]}
          then: k1p.box18a
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.has_b18a, {const: false}]}
          then: k1p.h_zero
    cite: "i1065sk1 2025 p.25, Box 18, code A"

  - id: k1p.box20z_if_any
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 20, code Z, qualified business income (loss), zero if blank"
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.has_b20z, {const: true}]}
          then: k1p.box20z
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.has_b20z, {const: false}]}
          then: k1p.h_zero
    cite: "i1065sk1 2025 pp.29-30, Box 20, code Z"

  - id: k1p.prior_total
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Total prior-year unallowed losses from this partnership"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [k1p.h_prior_b1, k1p.h_prior_b2, k1p.h_prior_b3]}
    cite: "i8582 2025 p.10, Parts IV and V, column (c)"

  - id: k1p.passive_loss
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This partnership has an overall loss from a passive activity (report using Form 8582)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, gt: [k1p.b1_overall_loss, {const: 0}]}
        - {op: test, gt: [k1p.b2_overall_loss, {const: 0}]}
        - {op: test, gt: [k1p.b3_overall_loss, {const: 0}]}
        - {op: test, gt: [k1p.h_b2_split, {const: 0}]}
    cite: "i1065sk1 2025 p.9, Effect of determination (overall loss)"

  - id: k1p.nonpassive_net
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Net nonpassive income (loss) from this partnership (Schedule E, line 28, column (k) less column (i))"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [k1p.h_b4a, k1p.h_b1_np_amt]}
    cite: "i1065sk1 2025 p.14, Box 1 item 1; p.15, Box 4a"

  - id: k1p.sche_row
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This partnership needs a row on Schedule E, line 28"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.has_b1, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.has_b2, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.has_b3, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.has_b4a, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.prior_any, {const: true}]}
    cite: "i1040se 2025 p.10, Line 28; i1065sk1 2025 p.14 (each partnership's K-1 on a separate line 28)"

  - id: k1p.screened
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This Schedule K-1 has items the tool does not support"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.ptp, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.r_b4b, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.r_b7, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.r_b10, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.r_b11, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.r_b12, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.r_b13, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.r_b14bc, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.r_b15, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.r_b16, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.r_b17, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.r_b20, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.r_b21, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.r_b2223, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.r_foreign, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.r_disposed, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_dist_gate, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_z_gate, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_loss_gate, {const: true}]}
    cite: "i1065sk1 2025, Limitations on Losses, Deductions, and Credits; Specific Instructions, boxes 4b, 7, 10 to 17, 19 to 23; item D"
```
